❯ # MyBhagya — Coin Wallet, Premium AI Tools & Astrologer Chat Billing          
                                                                                
  ## Objective                                                                  
                                                                                
  Implement a production-ready coin-based billing system in the existing        
  MyBhagya platform.                                                            
                                                                                
  The system must support:                                                      
                                                                                
  - One-time signup rewards.                                                    
  - A centralized user coin wallet.                                             
  - Free non-AI tools.                                                          
  - Coin-based premium AI tools.                                                
  - A single lifetime free-chat allowance across all astrologers.               
  - Fractional, per-second astrologer consultation billing.                     
  - Future Razorpay coin purchases.                                             
  - Superadmin pricing and wallet management.                                   
  - Complete financial auditability.                                            
                                                                                
  **First audit the existing codebase, inspect the provided spreadsheet, verify 
  AI provider pricing and prepare an implementation plan. Do not start coding   
  until I approve the plan.**                                                   
                                                                                
  ## 1. Existing system and constraints                                         
                                                                                
  Our existing platform uses React/Vite, Express/TypeScript, Neon PostgreSQL    
  and Prisma.                                                                   
                                                                                
  We already have:                                                              
                                                                                
  - USER, ADMIN and SUPERADMIN authentication.                                  
  - ASTROLOGER authorization and onboarding functionality.                      
  - User and astrologer dashboards.                                             
  - A basic chat system between astrologers and users.                          
  - Multiple astrology tools, including AI-powered features.                    
  - Existing integrations with AI providers.                                    
  - A Superadmin management panel.                                              
                                                                                
  Inspect the actual repository before assuming that every component is         
  complete.                                                                     
                                                                                
  Do not remove, rewrite or break existing working functionality.               
                                                                                
  Preserve existing authentication, role mappings, chat history, database       
  records and dashboards.                                                       
                                                                                
  ## 2. Finalized business rules                                                
                                                                                
  The following rules are final and must not be changed or reinterpreted.       
                                                                                
  ### Rule A — Coin value                                                       
                                                                                
  1 coin = ₹1 of in-platform spending value.                                    
                                                                                
  Store all wallet balances and charges as integer subunits.                    
                                                                                
  1 coin = 100 subunits.                                                        
                                                                                
  Examples:                                                                     
                                                                                
  - 50 coins = 5,000 subunits.                                                  
  - 7 coins = 700 subunits.                                                     
  - 5.02 coins = 502 subunits.                                                  
                                                                                
  Use integer arithmetic for all financial calculations.                        
                                                                                
  Coins are platform spending credits, not withdrawable cash.                   
                                                                                
  ### Rule B — Signup reward                                                    
                                                                                
  Every eligible newly registered user receives exactly 50 promotional coins    
  once.                                                                         
                                                                                
  Requirements:                                                                 
                                                                                
  - Grant automatically through the existing registration system.               
  - Grant exactly once per eligible account.                                    
  - Enforce uniqueness at database level.                                       
  - Prevent duplicate grants from retries, concurrent requests or repeated      
  registration events.                                                          
  - Do not automatically grant the reward to existing users.                    
  - Track the grant in an immutable wallet ledger.                              
  - Keep purchased and promotional coin balances separately identifiable.       
  - Promotional coins cannot be withdra                                         
  ──── (373 lines hidden) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── 
   provider cost monitoring.                                                    
                                                                                
  The finalized signup reward and free-chat policies should be implemented as   
  specified. Do not silently change them through unrelated configuration.       
                                                                                
  All manual wallet adjustments require authorization, a reason and an audit    
  record.                                                                       
                                                                                
  ## 12. Mandatory testing                                                      
                                                                                
  Include automated tests for:                                                  
                                                                                
  - Exactly 50 coins on eligible first signup.                                  
  - No duplicate signup rewards.                                                
  - Bonus coins usable for AI tools.                                            
  - Bonus coins usable for astrologer consultations.                            
  - One global 60-second free allowance.                                        
  - Free time carrying over between astrologers.                                
  - No reset on refresh or reconnect.                                           
  - No simultaneous double-consumption of free time.                            
  - Correct 43-second fractional billing.                                       
  - Correct cumulative rounding.                                                
  - No duplicate incremental charges.                                           
  - Insufficient-balance handling.                                              
  - Concurrent wallet spending.                                                 
  - Failed AI tool execution refunds.                                           
  - Backend restart recovery.                                                   
  - Unauthorized wallet access.                                                 
  - Unauthorized chat access.                                                   
  - Rate changes during active consultations.                                   
  - Payment webhook idempotency.                                                
  - Existing feature regression.                                                
                                                                                
  Use real PostgreSQL integration tests for transaction and concurrency         
  behavior.                                                                     
                                                                                
  ## 13. Implementation phases                                                  
                                                                                
  Phase 1: Repository audit, spreadsheet analysis, official pricing             
  verification and architecture plan.                                           
                                                                                
  Phase 2: Wallet database, ledger, signup reward and reservation services.     
                                                                                
  Phase 3: Premium AI tool pricing and billing integration.                     
                                                                                
  Phase 4: Chat architecture fixes, global free-minute allowance and fractional 
  billing.                                                                      
                                                                                
  Phase 5: User wallet UI, chat billing UI and Superadmin pricing controls.     
                                                                                
  Phase 6: Razorpay-ready payment architecture.                                 
                                                                                
  Phase 7: End-to-end testing, reconciliation and deployment verification.      
                                                                                
  ## 14. Required response before coding                                        
                                                                                
  First provide:                                                                
                                                                                
  1. Existing system audit.                                                     
  2. Chat flaws and recommended fixes.                                          
  3. Spreadsheet cost analysis.                                                 
  4. Verified AI provider pricing.                                              
  5. Proposed coin prices.                                                      
  6. Wallet and ledger schema.                                                  
  7. Global free-chat allowance implementation.                                 
  8. Fractional billing calculations.                                           
  9. Chat session state machine.                                                
  10. Premium tool billing flow.                                                
  11. Database migrations.                                                      
  12. Backend API design.                                                       
  13. Frontend changes.                                                         
  14. Superadmin changes.                                                       
  15. Razorpay compatibility.                                                   
  16. Concurrency and recovery strategy.                                        
  17. Security risks.                                                           
  18. Test plan.                                                                
  19. Implementation checkpoints.                                               
  20. Any genuine unresolved business decisions.                                
                                                                                
  Clearly distinguish confirmed requirements from technical recommendations.    
                                                                                
  **Stop after preparing the plan and wait for my approval. Do not start coding 
  yet.**

     │ MyBhagya Coin Wallet, Premium AI Tools & Astrologer Chat Billing —      │
     │ Architecture Plan                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Context                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ MyBhagya needs a coin-denominated billing layer covering premium AI     │
     │ tools and astrologer chat, with a one-time signup bonus, a single       │
     │ lifetime 60-second free-chat allowance platform-wide, per-second        │
     │ fractional chat billing, and a Razorpay-ready payment architecture —    │
     │ all auditable and superadmin-manageable.                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The repository is not greenfield for this. There is an in-progress,     │
     │ partially-complete rupee/paise wallet + chat billing system already     │
     │ built (untracked backend/src/modules/chat/,                             │
     │ backend/src/modules/payment/, backend/src/realtime/,                    │
     │ frontend/src/features/chat/, frontend/src/features/wallet/, plus the    │
     │ applied migration 20261107130000_add_wallet_payment_chat). This plan    │
     │ extends and corrects that system rather than building a parallel one —  │
     │ confirmed with you: rename/extend in place, since                       │
     │ balancePaise/amountPaise are already integer subunits and 1 coin = ₹1 = │
     │ 100 subunits means no unit conversion is needed, only a presentation    │
     │ rename and new capabilities layered on top.                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The two most important deltas from what exists today:                   │
     │ 1. Free-chat allowance is currently 7 minutes per astrologer            │
     │ (ChatFreeTrialUsage, unique per (userId, astrologerProfileId)) — Rule D │
     │ requires 60 seconds once, globally, across the whole platform. This     │
     │ must be redesigned, not just relabeled.                                 │
     │ 2. Chat billing rounds per 5-second tick independently (Math.round in   │
     │ billing.ts), not cumulatively — Rule E requires ceil applied to         │
     │ cumulative billable seconds minus previously-settled charge, to avoid   │
     │ rounding drift and never recompute the same interval.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Everything else (wallet ledger shape, transactional credit/debit,       │
     │ crash-recovery via lastBilledAt, snapshot-the-rate-at-session-start,    │
     │ socket room/auth pattern) is sound and will be reused as-is or extended │
     │ additively.                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 1. Existing system audit                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Wallet / Payment (backend/prisma/schema/payment.prisma,                 │
     │ backend/src/modules/payment/)                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Wallet (userId @unique, balancePaise Int), WalletTransaction          │
     │ (append-only ledger: type CREDIT|DEBIT, reason                          │
     │ RECHARGE|CHAT_CONSUMPTION|REFUND|ADJUSTMENT, amountPaise, denormalized  │
     │ balanceAfterPaise, loosely-typed referenceId string, createdAt). Real,  │
     │ working, transaction-safe (prisma.$transaction), balance clamped ≥0 in  │
     │ debit() (wallet.service.ts).                                            │
     │ - PaymentOrder (amountPaise, provider MOCK|RAZORPAY, status             │
     │ CREATED|SUCCESS|FAILED, providerOrderId, providerPaymentId,             │
     │ completedAt) — create→confirm shape mirrors a real gateway,             │
     │ confirmRechargeOrder is already idempotent against retried confirms.    │
     │ payment.provider.ts is a pure mock (mockPaymentProvider, instant fake   │
     │ order id, always succeeds) — explicitly documented as swappable:        │
     │ "implement this same interface and swap the export... nothing in        │
     │ payment.service.ts or the wallet/chat modules needs to change."         │
     │ PaymentProvider.RAZORPAY is an unused enum literal; zero Razorpay       │
     │ SDK/webhook/signature code exists anywhere.                             │
     │ - WalletTransactionReason.ADJUSTMENT exists in the schema with no code  │
     │ path that ever constructs one — confirmed gap, this is where superadmin │
     │ manual adjustments will hook in.                                        │
     │ - MIN_RECHARGE_PAISE = 2000 (₹20), MAX_RECHARGE_PAISE = 1,000,000       │
     │ (₹10,000) in payment.service.ts.                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Chat / Consultation (backend/src/modules/chat/,                         │
     │ backend/src/realtime/socketServer.ts,                                   │
     │ backend/prisma/schema/chat.prisma)                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Lifecycle today: ACTIVE → ENDED only. startSession                    │
     │ (chat.service.ts:88) creates the session already ACTIVE the instant the │
     │ user calls POST /chat/sessions — there is no REQUESTED/ACCEPTED         │
     │ handshake, no astrologer accept/reject, and billing                     │
     │ (billing.registerSession) starts before the astrologer has seen         │
     │ anything. TIMEOUT is a declared ChatEndReason that nothing ever         │
     │ triggers. Resuming an existing ACTIVE session for the same (userId,     │
     │ astrologerProfileId) is the one crash-recovery/multi-tab safeguard      │
     │ already in place (dedupe on session creation, not on socket join).      │
     │ - Billing (billing.ts): a setInterval per session (BILLING_TICK_MS =    │
     │ 5000) recomputes elapsedSeconds = now - lastBilledAt every tick —       │
     │ crash-safe (resumeAllActiveSessions() re-registers all ACTIVE sessions  │
     │ from lastBilledAt on server start, called non-blocking before           │
     │ httpServer.listen). Rounding bug relative to Rule E: each tick computes │
     │ costPaise = Math.round(elapsedSeconds * ratePerMinutePaise / 60)        │
     │ independently and accumulates — this must become                        │
     │ ceil(cumulativeBilledSeconds * rate / 60) - amountPaiseAlreadySettled   │
     │ per Rule E.                                                             │
     │ - Free trial: FREE_TRIAL_SECONDS = 7 * 60, enforced via                 │
     │ ChatFreeTrialUsage @@unique([userId, astrologerProfileId]) —            │
     │ per-astrologer, not global. Must be replaced by a single global         │
     │ allowance (Section 7).                                                  │
     │ - ChatSession.ratePerMinutePaise is snapshotted at session start —      │
     │ already satisfies "rate changes never retroactively affect an active    │
     │ session."                                                               │
     │ - Realtime: JWT-authenticated sockets (socketServer.ts), per-session    │
     │ room (chat:${sessionId}) joined on-demand after an access check,        │
     │ per-astrologer room (astrologer:${id}) auto-joined, presence via        │
     │ connection-count + 10s grace window (conflicts with the separate manual │
     │ PATCH /astrologer/chat/online toggle — two uncoded-together writers of  │
     │ the same two columns). Message persist-then-broadcast (no in-memory     │
     │ buffer). No reconnection catch-up protocol, no heartbeat tuning, no     │
     │ multi-tab coordination, no transport-level idempotency key (UI dedupes  │
     │ incoming messages by server id only).                                   │
     │ - Public pricing exposure (astrologerPublic.route.ts): pricePerMinute   │
     │ (rupees, nullable — null means "not set," never fake-zero) returned on  │
     │ GET /api/astrologers / /:slug.                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Auth / Registration (backend/prisma/schema/auth.prisma,                 │
     │ backend/src/modules/auth/auth.service.ts)                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - User has no balance/coin field and no signup-bonus flag. Roles are a  │
     │ Role/UserRoleMapping lookup table, not an enum.                         │
     │ - register() creates the user + role mapping in one prisma.$transaction │
     │ (line ~129) but the account is not yet verified at this point           │
     │ (isEmailVerified: false). The actual "account activated" moment is in   │
     │ verifyOtp() (line 254), specifically the data.type === "REGISTER" &&    │
     │ !user.isEmailVerified branch (line 314) — today a bare                  │
     │ prisma.user.update, not wrapped in a transaction. This is the correct   │
     │ hook point for the one-time signup bonus (Section 6), and it must       │
     │ become transactional.                                                   │
     │ - Google sign-in (loginWithGoogleProfile, line ~479) creates an         │
     │ already-verified user inside its own prisma.$transaction (line ~520)    │
     │ when no existing account is found — second hook point, same             │
     │ transaction.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ AI integrations — no direct provider SDKs                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ All LLM calls go through OpenRouter or Groq HTTP APIs (both             │
     │ OpenAI-compatible, called via raw axios), plus Sarvam (TTS) and         │
     │ ElevenLabs (narration audio). No openai, @anthropic-ai/sdk, or          │
     │ @google/generative-ai package exists. A real, already-built AI cost     │
     │ registry exists at backend/src/modules/admin/aiFeatureCost.ts           │
     │ (OPERATIONS[], FREE_OPERATIONS[], REFERENCE_RATES) driven by a          │
     │ usage_events table that records actual provider-reported or             │
     │ token-estimated cost per request — this is the exact foundation Rule G  │
     │ asks for ("keep actual AI provider costs separate from customer-facing  │
     │ prices") and the plan reuses it rather than building a second registry. │
     │ The supplied spreadsheet                                                │
     │ (MyBhagya_Astro_AI_Feature_Cost_Breakdown.xlsx) turns out to be         │
     │ generated directly from this file's REFERENCE_RATES/OPERATIONS (same    │
     │ models, same verifiedAt: 2026-10-31), not an independent source — see   │
     │ Section 4.                                                              │
     │ - A near-perfect concurrency template for coin reservations already     │
     │ exists: ToolLimit/ToolUsageAttempt (toolLimit.prisma) — admits a        │
     │ request by inserting a row, deletes it on a 5xx to "refund" the quota   │
     │ slot. The premium-tool reservation flow (Section 7) follows the same    │
     │ admit-then-settle-or-release shape, applied to coins instead of a       │
     │ request count.                                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Frontend                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - frontend/src/features/chat/ is fully built and wired: socket client,  │
     │ REST API client, useChatSession hook (live timer/balance via            │
     │ chat:timer), ChatWindow, StartChatButton (eligibility check →           │
     │ recharge-or-start), astrologer-side conversation inbox and online       │
     │ toggle, all already shipped UI this plan must not break.                │
     │ - frontend/src/features/wallet/ exists (walletApi, useWallet,           │
     │ RechargeModal with explicit "payments are simulated" copy) but nothing  │
     │ renders a wallet page or transaction history — useWallet() is currently │
     │ unused outside the recharge flow.                                       │
     │ - authStore / AuthUser has no balance field at all — a coin balance     │
     │ needs either a new store slice or a globally-cached wallet query        │
     │ (recommend the latter, reusing existing React Query setup, to avoid     │
     │ balance drift between the store and the ledger).                        │
     │ - None of the AI tool pages (horoscope, compatibility, birth chart,     │
     │ name-destiny, palm reading, tarot, inner-voice, ratna-jyotish) show any │
     │ price/cost today — this is greenfield frontend work.                    │
     │ - Superadmin is not a separate app — it's the single /admin/* tree      │
     │ gated by SUPERADMIN/SUPER_ADMIN role (AdminRoute.tsx, mirrored          │
     │ backend-side by requireMainAdmin). An existing payments admin page and  │
     │ chat-escalations page are candidates to extend; no wallet/coin admin    │
     │ page exists.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 2. Chat architecture flaws and recommended fixes                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ #: 1                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: No REQUESTED/ACCEPTED state — billing starts the instant the user │
     │ clicks                                                                  │
     │ start, astrologer can't decline                                         │
     │ Fix: Add REQUESTED → astrologer must POST /sessions/:id/accept or       │
     │ /reject                                                                 │
     │ within a timeout before ACTIVE begins; billing registration moves to    │
     │ the                                                                     │
     │ transition into ACTIVE                                                  │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 2                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: TIMEOUT end-reason is declared but never fires                    │
     │ Fix: Add a server-side timer on REQUESTED sessions (e.g. 60s) that      │
     │ auto-transitions to REJECTED-equivalent with endedReason: TIMEOUT       │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 3                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: No guard against one astrologer having two simultaneous sessions  │
     │ with                                                                    │
     │ different users                                                         │
     │ Fix: Add a uniqueness check (DB-enforced where feasible): an astrologer │
     │ may                                                                     │
     │ have at most one REQUESTED/ACTIVE session at a time                     │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 4                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: Per-tick independent rounding (Math.round per 5s chunk)           │
     │ Fix: Replace with cumulative ceil minus previously-settled, per Rule E  │
     │ (Section                                                                │
     │ 8)                                                                      │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 5                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: Free allowance is per-astrologer, not global                      │
     │ Fix: Replace ChatFreeTrialUsage consumption model with a single global  │
     │ allowance row per user, atomically decremented (Section 7)              │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 6                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: Presence has two uncoordinated writers (socket grace-window vs.   │
     │ manual                                                                  │
     │ PATCH /online)                                                          │
     │ Fix: Keep manual toggle as the astrologer's intent; let the             │
     │ socket-driven value                                                     │
     │  only downgrade "intent=online" to "actually offline" after the grace   │
     │ window,                                                                 │
     │  never upgrade it — store both fields distinctly if needed, or document │
     │                                                                         │
     │ precedence rules in code                                                │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 7                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: No reconnection catch-up / no multi-tab coordination              │
     │ Fix: Out of scope for billing correctness (billing is already           │
     │ server-authoritative and crash-safe); add a "fetch messages since       │
     │ last-seen                                                               │
     │ cursor" on chat:join as a UX improvement, not a billing requirement     │
     │ ────────────────────────────────────────                                │
     │ #: 8                                                                    │
     │ Flaw: WalletTransaction.referenceId is a loose string, not a real FK    │
     │ Fix: Keep as-is (matches existing convention across                     │
     │ PaymentOrder/ChatSession                                                │
     │ references) but add a walletReservationId FK-typed column for the new   │
     │ reservation flow specifically, since that one needs real joins for      │
     │ crash                                                                   │
     │ recovery                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ This plan does not rebuild chat messaging, sockets, or the UI — only    │
     │ the session-lifecycle state machine, the billing rounding/reservation   │
     │ logic, and the free-allowance source of truth change.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 3. Wallet and ledger schema (additive, Prisma)                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Reusing Wallet/WalletTransaction as the one ledger (paise subunit ==    │
     │ coin subunit, 1:1, no conversion). Additive changes to payment.prisma:  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ prisma                                                                  │
     │ model Wallet {                                                          │
     │   // existing fields unchanged                                          │
     │   balancePaise            Int @default(0) // total available =          │
     │ purchased + promotional                                                 │
     │   purchasedBalancePaise    Int @default(0) // new: portion from         │
     │ Razorpay top-ups                                                        │
     │   promotionalBalancePaise  Int @default(0) // new: portion from signup  │
     │ bonus / future promos                                                   │
     │   reservedBalancePaise     Int @default(0) // new: held for in-flight   │
     │ premium-tool executions                                                 │
     │   reservations WalletReservation[]                                      │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ enum WalletTransactionReason {                                          │
     │   RECHARGE                                                              │
     │   CHAT_CONSUMPTION                                                      │
     │   REFUND                                                                │
     │   ADJUSTMENT                                                            │
     │   SIGNUP_BONUS        // new                                            │
     │   TOOL_EXECUTION      // new                                            │
     │   RESERVATION_RELEASE // new                                            │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ enum WalletBalanceBucket { PURCHASED PROMOTIONAL }                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ model WalletTransaction {                                               │
     │   // existing fields unchanged, plus:                                   │
     │   bucket            WalletBalanceBucket // new: which balance this      │
     │ entry moved                                                             │
     │   idempotencyKey    String?             @unique // new: dedupes retried │
     │ billing/webhook writes                                                  │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /// One-time signup bonus grant — the unique constraint IS the "exactly │
     │ once" enforcement.                                                      │
     │ model SignupBonusGrant {                                                │
     │   id        String   @id @default(cuid())                               │
     │   userId    String   @unique                                            │
     │   amountPaise Int                                                       │
     │   grantedAt DateTime @default(now())                                    │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /// Database-backed hold for a premium AI tool execution or a short     │
     │ chat-billing lookahead window.                                          │
     │ model WalletReservation {                                               │
     │   id           String   @id @default(cuid())                            │
     │   walletId     String                                                   │
     │   wallet       Wallet   @relation(fields: [walletId], references: [id]) │
     │   amountPaise  Int                                                      │
     │   purpose      WalletReservationPurpose // TOOL_EXECUTION |             │
     │ CHAT_LOOKAHEAD                                                          │
     │   referenceId  String   // ToolExecution.id or ChatSession.id           │
     │   status       WalletReservationStatus @default(HELD) // HELD | SETTLED │
     │ | RELEASED                                                              │
     │   createdAt    DateTime @default(now())                                 │
     │   resolvedAt   DateTime?                                                │
     │   @@index([walletId, status])                                           │
     │   @@index([referenceId])                                                │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Lifetime free-chat allowance (new model, replaces per-astrologer        │
     │ ChatFreeTrialUsage as the gating mechanism — see Section 7):            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ prisma                                                                  │
     │ model ChatFreeAllowance {                                               │
     │   userId            String @id                                          │
     │   totalSeconds      Int    @default(60)                                 │
     │   consumedSeconds   Int    @default(0)                                  │
     │   updatedAt         DateTime @updatedAt                                 │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ChatFreeTrialUsage is kept in the schema (historical data, no           │
     │ destructive drop) but stops being read by getEligibility/startSession   │
     │ once the global allowance ships.                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Premium AI tool pricing/billing (new models):                           │

     │   @@index([userId, status])                                             │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Payment orders (extend existing PaymentOrder, no breaking change):      │

     │ All new migrations are additive (new tables/columns/enums only) —       │
     │ consistent with the existing convention in astrologer.prisma's own      │
     │ header comment ("Additive-only domain — nothing here changes an         │

     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 5. Global free-chat allowance (Rule D)                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - New ChatFreeAllowance row, created lazily (upsert, like Wallet) on    │
     │ first eligibility check, totalSeconds: 60.                              │
     │ - getEligibility() and startSession() in chat.service.ts change from    │
     │ "check ChatFreeTrialUsage for this astrologer" to "read                 │
     │ ChatFreeAllowance.consumedSeconds for this user" — if consumedSeconds < │
     │ totalSeconds, the session type reported to the frontend becomes a       │
     │ hybrid: free seconds first, then paid, within the same session (not a   │
     │ separate FREE_TRIAL session type per astrologer as today).              │
     │ - Atomicity: the free-seconds consumption happens inside the same       │
     │ per-tick transaction as billing (Section 8), via a conditional update   │
     │ (UPDATE chat_free_allowances SET consumed_seconds = consumed_seconds +  │
     │ :n WHERE user_id = :id AND consumed_seconds + :n <= total_seconds) —    │
     │ this prevents two concurrent active sessions (if ever possible) from    │
     │ double-spending the same free seconds, and is the DB-level analog of    │
     │ "prefer preventing multiple simultaneous active consultations for the   │
     │ same user," which this plan also enforces at the session-start layer    │
     │ (Section 2, flaw #3, extended to users too: a user may have at most one │
     │ ACTIVE/REQUESTED session at a time, platform-wide).                     │
     │ - Unused free seconds persist indefinitely across                       │
     │ astrologers/devices/sessions — no reset logic exists anywhere, by       │
     │ construction (nothing ever recreates the row or increments totalSeconds │
     │ beyond the one-time 60).                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 6. Signup reward (Rule B/C)                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Hook point: auth.service.ts verifyOtp(), the data.type === "REGISTER" │
     │ && !user.isEmailVerified branch (currently a bare prisma.user.update)   │
     │ — wrap in prisma.$transaction:                                          │
     │ await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {                               │
     │   await tx.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: {                │
     │ isEmailVerified: true } });                                             │
     │   try {                                                                 │
     │     await tx.signupBonusGrant.create({ data: { userId: user.id,         │
     │ amountPaise: 5000 } }); // 50 coins = 5000 subunits                     │
     │     const wallet = await tx.wallet.upsert({ where: { userId: user.id }, │
     │ update: {}, create: { userId: user.id, balancePaise: 0 } });            │
     │     await tx.wallet.update({ where: { id: wallet.id }, data: {          │
     │ balancePaise: { increment: 5000 }, promotionalBalancePaise: {           │
     │ increment: 5000 } } });                                                 │
     │     await tx.walletTransaction.create({ data: { walletId: wallet.id,    │
     │ type: "CREDIT", reason: "SIGNUP_BONUS", bucket: "PROMOTIONAL",          │
     │ amountPaise: 5000, balanceAfterPaise: wallet.balancePaise + 5000,       │
     │ idempotencyKey: `signup-bonus:${user.id}` } });                         │
     │   } catch (e) {                                                         │
     │     if (!isUniqueConstraintError(e)) throw e; //                        │
     │ SignupBonusGrant.userId unique — retries/concurrent calls no-op here    │
     │   }                                                                     │
     │ });                                                                     │
     │ - The SignupBonusGrant.userId @unique constraint is the actual "exactly │
     │ once" guarantee — the try/catch on a unique-violation makes             │
     │ concurrent/retried OTP verifications idempotent without a second DB     │
     │ round-trip to check first.                                              │
     │ - Same transaction block added to loginWithGoogleProfile()'s existing   │
     │ tx.user.create branch (new-user path only — existing users never get    │
     │ it, per Rule B).                                                        │
     │ - Not granted to already-existing users — the grant only ever happens   │
     │ inside the two "this is a brand-new account becoming active" code       │
     │ paths, never as a backfill.                                             │
     │ - Spending priority (Rule C): every debit (chat billing tick,           │
     │ tool-execution settlement) first decrements promotionalBalancePaise     │
     │ (floored at 0), then purchasedBalancePaise, for the same total          │
     │ amountPaise — and the WalletTransaction.bucket field records which      │
     │ bucket actually funded it, so a refund can credit back to the same      │
     │ bucket it came from.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 7. Fractional chat billing (Rule E) — corrected algorithm               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Replace billing.ts's per-tick Math.round with cumulative ceil:          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ // On each tick, for a PAID (or hybrid free+paid) session:              │
     │ const totalElapsedSeconds = session.billedSeconds + elapsedSeconds;     │
     │ // cumulative, never reset                                              │
     │ const freeSecondsUsedThisTick = Math.min(elapsedSeconds,                │
     │ remainingFreeAllowance);                                                │
     │ const billableSecondsCumulative = totalElapsedSeconds -                 │
     │ totalFreeSecondsEverApplied; // only paid seconds count toward the      │
     │ charge                                                                  │
     │ const cumulativeChargeDue = Math.ceil(billableSecondsCumulative *       │
     │ ratePerMinutePaise / 60);                                               │
     │ const newCharge = cumulativeChargeDue - session.amountPaise; //         │
     │ amountPaise = previously settled, monotonic                             │
     │ // debit(wallet, newCharge, ...) only for newCharge > 0; never          │
     │ re-derive or re-round past ticks.                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ This satisfies: 1s=0.12, 13s=1.52, 30s=3.50, 43s=5.02, 60s=7.00 at 7    │
     │ coins/min, verified by direct computation (ceil(n*700/60)/100for        │
     │ n=1,13,30,43,60 → 12,152,350,502,700 subunits). Free and paid time are  │
     │ subtracted in the correct order (Rule E's "subtract remaining free      │
     │ allowance before calculating paid duration") by applying free seconds   │
     │ first out of elapsedSeconds each tick, decrementing                     │
     │ ChatFreeAllowance.consumedSeconds atomically (Section 5) before         │
     │ computing billableSecondsCumulative.                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Settlement remains tick-based (every 5s) for responsiveness, but the    │
     │ charge is always "cumulative formula minus already-settled," never an   │
     │ independently-rounded per-tick amount — eliminating rounding drift      │
     │ entirely.                                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 8. Wallet reservations (Rule G + chat lookahead)                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Premium AI tools (PRICE_QUOTED → COINS_RESERVED → EXECUTING → COMPLETED │
     │ → SETTLED, with FAILED/CANCELLED/RELEASED/RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED):     │
     │ 1. Client requests a quote: server reads ToolPrice.coinPriceSubunits    │
     │ (current active version), returns it with a server-signed quote (short  │
     │ TTL, re-validated server-side at reservation time — never trusts a      │
     │ client-echoed price).                                                   │
     │ 2. Client confirms execution with a client-generated idempotency key.   │
     │ Server: begins prisma.$transaction, re-validates the quote is still     │
     │ current, checks wallet.balancePaise - wallet.reservedBalancePaise >=    │
     │ price, creates WalletReservation(HELD) + increments                     │
     │ Wallet.reservedBalancePaise, creates ToolExecution(COINS_RESERVED)      │
     │ keyed by the idempotency key (unique constraint — a retried request     │
     │ with the same key returns the existing execution instead of             │
     │ double-reserving).                                                      │
     │ 3. Server calls the AI provider. On success: ToolExecution → COMPLETED, │
     │ then in one transaction: debit the reserved amount from the correct     │
     │ bucket (promotional-first per Rule C), mark WalletReservation(SETTLED), │
     │ decrement reservedBalancePaise, ToolExecution → SETTLED. Separately,    │
     │ the existing usage_events row (actual provider cost) is linked via      │
     │ providerUsageEventId — this is how actual AI cost stays separate from   │
     │ the customer-facing coin price (Rule G).                                │
     │ 4. On provider failure/timeout: release the reservation                 │
     │ (WalletReservation → RELEASED, reservedBalancePaise decremented, no     │
     │ debit), ToolExecution → FAILED. On an uncertain outcome (provider       │
     │ timeout with unknown completion state): ToolExecution →                 │
     │ RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED — never automatically retried, per your "never  │
     │ silently repeat an uncertain billable provider request" requirement; a  │
     │ reconciliation job or admin action resolves it later.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Chat billing does not need a multi-second lookahead reservation to be   │
     │ correct (Section 7's cumulative-ceil-minus-settled already prevents     │
     │ double-charging and the tick interval is only 5s), but it does reuse    │
     │ WalletReservation with purpose: CHAT_LOOKAHEAD for one thing: at        │
     │ startSession, reserve MIN_BILLABLE_SECONDS worth of coins (already      │
     │ checked today via minimumRequiredPaise, just not reified as a           │
     │ reservation row) so that balance cannot be spent elsewhere (e.g., a     │
     │ premium tool call from another tab) while a chat session the user just  │
     │ started is about to begin billing it.                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Crash recovery: WalletReservation rows are durable and queried on       │
     │ startup — add a reconcileStaleReservations() step alongside the         │
     │ existing resumeAllActiveSessions() in index.ts's startup sequence: any  │
     │ HELD reservation older than a timeout with no corresponding             │
     │ COMPLETED/FAILED ToolExecution is flagged RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED,      │
     │ never silently released or silently settled.                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 9. Chat session state machine (final)                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ REQUESTED --(astrologer accepts, within timeout)--> ACTIVE --(end       │
     │ reasons below)--> ENDED                                                 │
     │ REQUESTED --(astrologer rejects | timeout expires)--> ENDED             │
     │ (endedReason: ASTROLOGER_ENDED-equivalent "REJECTED" | TIMEOUT)         │
     │ ACTIVE --(user/astrologer ends)--> ENDED (USER_ENDED |                  │
     │ ASTROLOGER_ENDED)                                                       │
     │ ACTIVE --(billing tick)--> ENDED (BALANCE_EXHAUSTED |                   │
     │ FREE_LIMIT_REACHED — only once both free allowance AND wallet are       │
     │ exhausted)                                                              │
     │ - Billing registration (billing.registerSession) moves from             │
     │ startSession to the REQUESTED→ACTIVE transition.                        │
     │ - ChatSessionStatus enum gains REQUESTED (additive enum value);         │
     │ existing ACTIVE/ENDED rows are unaffected.                              │
     │ - One ACTIVE/REQUESTED session per user platform-wide (new constraint,  │
     │ Section 5) and one ACTIVE/REQUESTED session per astrologer at a time    │
     │ (Section 2, flaw #3) — both enforced in the same                        │
     │ startSession/acceptSession transaction via a findFirst check before     │
     │ create (mirrors the existing same-pair dedupe pattern already in the    │
     │ code).                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 10. Premium tool billing flow                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Already detailed in Section 8. API surface:                             │
     │ - GET /api/tools/:toolKey/price → current ToolPrice (public, used to    │
     │ render the price before execution — Rule G).                            │
     │ - POST /api/tools/:toolKey/execute with header Idempotency-Key:         │
     │ <client-uuid> and the tool's normal payload → runs the full             │
     │ PRICE_QUOTED→SETTLED flow server-side in one call (synchronous tools);  │
     │ for any tool whose provider call can exceed a reasonable request        │
     │ timeout, split into POST .../reserve + POST .../confirm so the          │
     │ reservation survives a client disconnect.                               │
     │ - Every existing AI tool route (tarot, interpretation, birth-chart,     │
     │ compatibility, name-analysis, inner-voice, gemstone explanation) gets   │
     │ wrapped by a shared withCoinBilling(toolKey, handler)                   │
     │ middleware-equivalent, reusing the existing ToolLimit admit/refund      │
     │ pattern's shape but operating on coins instead of a request counter.    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ 11. Database migrations                                                 │
     │                                                                         │

     │                                                                         │
     │ No migration will be run against any database without your explicit     │

     │                                                                         │
     │ - All balance mutations go through wallet.service.ts's credit()/debit() │

     │ pattern.                                                                │
     │ - Reservations (WalletReservation) are the mechanism preventing         │
     │ double-spend between a premium tool call and a concurrently-ticking     │
     │ chat session — wallet.reservedBalancePaise is checked against           │
     │ balancePaise before any new reservation, inside the same transaction    │
     │ that creates it.                                                        │
     │ - ChatFreeAllowance consumption uses a single conditional UPDATE ...    │
     │ WHERE consumed_seconds + :n <= total_seconds to prevent two concurrent  │
     │ sessions over-consuming the global 60s (Section 5).                     │

     │ accepting traffic-affecting billing.                                    │
     │ - At most one ACTIVE/REQUESTED session per user and per astrologer,     │
     │ enforced transactionally at session-start/accept (Sections 2, 9).       │
     │                                                                         │

     │                                                                         │

     │ possible.                                                               │

     │ endpoint too — a wallet-adjustment endpoint must resolve "is this       │

     │ unverified call.                                                        │

✻ Cooked for 7m 19s

❯ I reviewed your MyBhagya Coin Wallet Architecture Plan.                       
                                                                                
  The overall approach is approved in principle: extend the existing            
  wallet/chat system rather than building a parallel one.                       
                                                                                
  However, fix the following implementation blockers before coding.             
                                                                                
  ### 1. Signup bonus concurrency                                               
                                                                                
  The proposed try/catch inside a Prisma interactive transaction is unsafe for  
  PostgreSQL unique-constraint errors.                                          
                                                                                
  Use a proper idempotent grant operation with database uniqueness and          
  transaction-safe conflict handling.                                           
                                                                                
  Do not swallow database errors or accidentally commit email verification      
  without the intended bonus when the bonus should be granted.                  
                                                                                
  Verify concurrent OTP confirmation and Google signup behavior.                
                                                                                
  ### 2. Existing wallet migration                                              
                                                                                
  Before adding purchased and promotional balances, audit existing wallet       
  balances, transactions and mock recharges.                                    
                                                                                
  Define how every existing balance will be classified.                         
                                                                                
  Preserve the invariant:                                                       
                                                                                
  totalBalance = purchasedBalance + promotionalBalance                          
                                                                                
  availableBalance = totalBalance - reservedBalance                             
                                                                                
  Provide a migration and reconciliation strategy. Do not initialize new bucket 
  balances to zero while leaving nonzero existing balances unexplained.         
                                                                                
  ### 3. Ledger allocations                                                     
                                                                                
  One bucket field per transaction is insufficient for charges funded by both   
  promotional and purchased coins.                                              
                                                                                
  Design a ledger that records split allocations under one billing operation.   
                                                                                
  Refunds must restore the exact original buckets.                              
                                                                                
  Every operation must be atomic and idempotent.                                
                                                                                
  ### 4. Chat reservations                                                      
                                                                                
  Reserving only the minimum interval at session start is insufficient.         
                                                                                
  Implement rolling reservations or another rigorously justified prepaid        
  billing mechanism that prevents the session from accruing charges beyond      
  available funds.                                                              
                                                                                
  Account for concurrent premium-tool executions, wallet reservations and       
  ongoing chat billing.                                                         
                                                                                
  ### 5. Chat timing and downtime                                               
                                                                                
  Do not automatically bill wall-clock time while the backend, user or          
  astrologer is disconnected.                                                   
                                                                                
  Define active presence, interruption handling, reconnection grace periods and 
  authoritative billable time.                                                  
                                                                                
  Crash recovery must not automatically treat server downtime as completed      
  consultation time.                                                            
                                                                                
  ### 6. Concurrent chat sessions                                               
                                                                                
  A findFirst check inside a transaction is not sufficient to prevent           
  concurrent session creation.                                                  
                                                                                
  Use PostgreSQL partial unique indexes, locking or another database-enforced   
  concurrency mechanism.                                                        
                                                                                
  Allow at most one active/requested consultation per user and per astrologer,  
  with the correct handling of pending requests.                                
                                                                                
  ### 7. Global free allowance migration                                        
                                                                                
  The old system provided seven minutes per astrologer.                         
                                                                                
  Do not silently reset historical free usage or automatically consume the new  
  allowance based on old records.                                               
                                                                                
  Report the number of affected users and propose a clear migration policy for  
  approval.                                                                     
                                                                                
  The new policy is exactly 60 seconds once per user across all astrologers,    
  with unused seconds carried forward.                                          
                                                                                
  ### 8. Premium AI tool integration                                            
                                                                                
  Do not assume a generic billing middleware can safely wrap every AI endpoint. 
                                                                                
  Inspect each route for streaming, caching, retries, provider timeouts and     
  asynchronous execution.                                                       
                                                                                
  Define per-operation idempotency and result-delivery semantics.               
                                                                                
  Avoid charging twice for retries or cached responses.                         
                                                                                
  ### 9. Mock payments                                                          
                                                                                
  Disable production-accessible mock recharge flows before enabling valuable    
  coins.                                                                        
                                                                                
  A mock payment confirmation must never allow an ordinary production user to   
  mint purchased coins.                                                         
                                                                                
  Razorpay remains a later integration.                                         
                                                                                
  ### 10. Pricing verification                                                  
                                                                                
  Provide official source URLs and verified calculations for all proposed       
  prices.                                                                       
                                                                                
  Separate actual measured provider usage from assumptions and maximum-output   
  estimates.                                                                    
                                                                                
  Do not activate unapproved prices.                                            
                                                                                
  ### 11. Finalized business decisions                                          
                                                                                
  - Signup reward: 50 promotional coins once per eligible new account.          
  - Coin value: 1 coin = ₹1 of platform spending value.                         
  - Internal precision: 100 integer subunits per coin.                          
  - Signup bonus usable for both premium AI tools and astrologer consultations. 
  - Free chat: 60 seconds once per user across all astrologers.                 
  - Unused free seconds carry forward.                                          
  - Paid chat: cumulative per-second billing, rounded upward to the nearest     
  0.01 coin.                                                                    
  - Request acceptance timeout: 60 seconds, no charge before activation.        
  - Dormant server-side Palm Reading AI: exclude from initial paid launch.      
  - Karma/AI Guru/Shloka: price each distinct billable execution, not merely    
  the shared endpoint.                                                          
  - Old automatic seven-minute per-astrologer trial: remove from the new        
  billing policy.                                                               
  - Explicitly approved zero-rate astrologers may offer free consultations      
  without consuming the global promotional allowance.                           
  - Tool prices require separate approval before activation.                    
                                                                                
  ### Required next output                                                      
                                                                                
  Revise the architecture plan to address these issues.                         
                                                                                
  Show:                                                                         
  1. Corrected Prisma models and constraints.                                   
  2. Existing wallet data migration strategy.                                   
  3. Signup grant idempotency.                                                  
  4. Wallet ledger and split-bucket settlement.                                 
  5. Rolling chat reservations.                                                 
  6. Session timing and disconnect policy.                                      
  7. Database-enforced session uniqueness.                                      
  8. Historical free-trial migration options.                                   
  9. Tool billing integration strategy.                                         
  10. Mock payment production safeguards.                                       
  11. Corrected tests and implementation checkpoints.                           
                                                                                
  Do not begin coding yet. Wait for my approval of the revised plan.
